src/app/api/faucet/route.ts24-39 public/tokens_devnet.json1-244 ## Environment Variables ### Required Environment Variables | Variable | Purpose | Example | | --- | --- | --- | | NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_ENV | Environment selection | localnet, devnet, mainnet | | NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL | Custom RPC endpoint | https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com | | NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL | Application base URL | https://app.stervault.com | | FAUCET_SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY | Faucet service keypair | JSON array of private key bytes | ### Environment 
- Specific Configuration The application uses different configuration loading strategies based on the environment.

In src/app/api/faucet/route.ts27-28 the token configuration URL is constructed dynamically: ``` const env = process.env.

NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_ENV || 'localnet'; const response = await fetch(`${process.env.

NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/tokens_${env}.json`); ``` RPC endpoint configuration is handled in src/app/api/faucet/route.ts48 with fallback to localhost: ``` const connectionUrl = process.env.

NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL || 'http://localhost:8899'; ``` ### Configuration Validation The cluster configuration includes validation for connection endpoints in src/components/cluster/cluster-data-access.tsx78-83: ``` addCluster: (cluster: Cluster) => { try { new Connection(cluster.endpoint) setClusters([...clusters, cluster]) } catch (err) { toast.error(`${err}`) } } ``` Sources: src/app/api/faucet/route.ts27-48 src/components/cluster/cluster-data-access.tsx78-83

## API Reference

# API Reference Relevant source files anchor/target/idl/lending.json anchor/target/types/lending.ts temp.ts This page provides comprehensive technical reference documentation for the Stervault lending program's API, including all instructions, account structures, error codes, and type definitions.

This covers the Solana program interface as defined in the Interface Definition Language (IDL).

For implementation details of React hooks and frontend components that consume this API, see Frontend API Reference.

For configuration values and constants, see Configuration and Constants. ## Program Overview The Stervault lending program is deployed at address FYkahL7zxyc3cS9wwA8b45JUNKoHSw6R4Ln5t7GXU5oD and provides a complete DeFi lending protocol on Solana.

The program supports token deposits, withdrawals, borrowing against collateral, and loan repayment with integrated Pyth Network price feeds for real-time asset valuation. ``` interest-bearing s. | Parameter | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | amount | u64 | Amount of tokens to deposit | Required Accounts: * signer (writable, signer): User's wallet * mint: Token mint address * bank (writable, PDA): Bank account for the token * bankTokenAccount (writable, PDA): Bank's token account * userAccount (writable, PDA): User's token state account * userTokenAccount (writable, PDA): User's associated token account * userGlobalState (writable, PDA): User's global state account Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts360-537 anchor/target/idl/lending.json354-531 ### Withdraw Instruction The withdraw instruction allows users to withdraw their deposited tokens plus accrued interest. | Parameter | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | amount | u64 | Amount of tokens to withdraw | Required Accounts: * signer (writable, signer): User's wallet * mint: Token mint address * bank (writable, PDA): Bank account for the token * bankTokenAccount (writable, PDA): Bank's token account * userTokenState (writable, PDA): User's token state account * userAssociatedTokenAccount (writable, PDA): User's associated token account Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts1272-1421 anchor/target/idl/lending.json1266-1415 ### Borrow Instruction The borrow instruction enables users to borrow tokens against their collateral with real-time price validation. | Parameter | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | positionId | u64 | Unique identifier for the borrow position | | amount | u64 | Amount of tokens to borrow | Required Accounts: * signer (writable, signer): User's wallet * mintBorrow: Mint address of token to borrow * mintCollateral: Mint address of collateral token * bankBorrow (writable, PDA): Bank account for borrow token * bankCollateral (writable, PDA): Bank account for collateral token * borrowPosition (writable, PDA): Borrow position account * priceUpdateBorrowToken: Pyth price update for borrow token * priceUpdateCollateralToken: Pyth price update for collateral token 
* Additional accounts for user token states and associated token accounts Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts16-359 anchor/target/idl/lending.json10-353 ### Repay Instruction The repay instruction allows users to repay borrowed tokens and release collateral. | Parameter | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | positionId | u64 | Unique identifier for the borrow position | | amount | u64 | Amount of tokens to repay | Required Accounts: Similar to borrow instruction with additional validation of position ownership and collateral release logic.

Structure The UserTokenState account tracks a user's activity for a specific token. | Field | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | owner | pubkey | User's wallet address | | mintAddress | pubkey | Token mint address | | depositeds | u64 | User's deposited s | | collaterals | u64 | User's collateral s | | borroweds | u64 | User's borrowed s | | lastUpdatedDeposited | i64 | Last deposit update timestamp | | lastUpdatedBorrowed | i64 | Last borrow update timestamp | | lastUpdatedCollateral | i64 | Last collateral update timestamp | PDA Seeds: [signer, mint_address] Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts1867-1905 anchor/target/idl/lending.json1861-1899 ### BorrowPosition Account Structure The BorrowPosition account represents an individual borrowing position with specific collateral. | Field | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | positionId | u64 | Unique position identifier | | owner | pubkey | Position owner's wallet | | collateralMint | pubkey | Collateral token mint | | borrowMint | pubkey | Borrowed token mint | | collaterals | u64 | Amount of collateral s | | borroweds | u64 | Amount of borrowed s | | lastUpdated | i64 | Last update timestamp | | active | bool | Position active status | PDA Seeds: ["position", signer, mint_collateral, mint_borrow, position_id] Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts1671-1709 anchor/target/idl/lending.json1665-1703 ## Error Codes Reference The program defines 17 custom error codes for various failure conditions: | Code | Name | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | 6000 | BorrowAmountTooLarge | Borrow amount exceeds maximum allowed | | 6001 | WithdrawAmountExceedsCollateralValue | Withdrawal would exceed collateral value | | 6002 | OverWithdrawRequest | Attempting to withdraw more than deposited | | 6003 | MathOverflow | Mathematical operation overflow | | 6004 | OverBorrowRequest | Attempting to borrow more than position allows | | 6005 | OverRepayRequest | Attempting to repay more than owed | | 6006 | HealthyAccount | Account is healthy, liquidation not allowed | | 6007 | OverBorrowableAmount | Borrow amount exceeds borrowable limit | | 6008 | InvalidPriceFeed | Price feed data is invalid | | 6009 | InvalidDepositAmount | Deposit amount is invalid | | 6010 | InvalidWithdrawAmount | Withdraw amount is invalid | | 6011 | BorrowAmountTooSmall | Borrow amount below minimum threshold | | 6012 | StalePrice | Price data is too old | | 6013 | InsufficientLiquidity | Not enough liquidity in the pool | | 6014 | InsufficientFunds | User has insufficient funds | | 6015 | InsufficientCollateral | User has insufficient collateral | | 6016 | AlreadyRepayed | Position has already been repaid | Sources: anchor/target/types/lending.ts1503-1588 ## Program IDL Reference

# Program IDL Reference Relevant source files anchor/programs/lending/src/error.rs anchor/programs/lending/src/state.rs anchor/target/idl/lending.json anchor/target/types/lending.ts This document provides comprehensive technical reference documentation for the Stervault lending program's Interface Definition Language (IDL).

The IDL defines the complete programmatic interface including all instructions, account structures, data types, and error codes that developers need to interact with the lending protocol.

For information about how these program instructions are used in the frontend application, see Frontend API Reference.

For details about the underlying program implementation and business logic, see Program Instructions. ## Program Metadata The lending program is deployed at address FYkahL7zxyc3cS9wwA8b45JUNKoHSw6R4Ln5t7GXU5oD with version 0.1.0 following the Anchor IDL specification version 0.1.0.

init_user Initializes a user's global state account for the lending protocol.

Discriminator: [14, 51, 68, 159, 237, 78, 158, 102] Accounts: | Name | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | signer | Signer, Writable | The user wallet initializing the account | | user_global_state | PDA, Writable | User's global state account (seeds: ["user_global", signer]) | | system_program | Program | Solana System Program | Arguments: None ### init_user_token_state Creates a user's token-specific state account for tracking deposits, collateral, and borrows.

Discriminator: [93, 39, 255, 186, 239, 199, 197, 123] Accounts: | Name | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | signer | Signer, Writable | User wallet | | user_account | PDA, Writable | User token state (seeds: [signer, mint_address]) | | user_global_state | PDA, Writable | User's global state | | system_program | Program | Solana System Program | Arguments: * mint_address (pubkey): Token mint address ### init_bank Initializes a lending bank for a specific token with risk parameters.

Discriminator: [73, 111, 27, 243, 202, 129, 159, 80] Accounts: | Name | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | signer | Signer, Writable | Bank authority | | mint | Account | Token mint | | bank | PDA, Writable | Bank account (seeds: [mint]) | | bank_token_account | PDA, Writable | Bank's token vault (seeds: ["treasury", mint]) | | token_program | Program | SPL Token Program | | system_program | Program | Solana System Program | Arguments: * liquidation_threshold (u64): Liquidation threshold in basis points * liquidation_bonus (u64): Liquidation bonus in basis points * liquidation_close_factor (u64): Maximum liquidation percentage * max_ltv (u64): Maximum loan-to-value ratio * deposit_interest_rate (u64): Annual deposit interest rate * borrow_interest_rate (u64): Annual borrow interest rate * name (string): Bank display name * description (string): Bank description * deposit_fee (u64): Deposit fee in basis points * withdrawal_fee (u64): Withdrawal fee in basis points * min_deposit (u64): Minimum deposit amount * interest_accrual_period (i64): Interest compounding period ### deposit Deposits tokens into a lending bank to earn interest.

Discriminator: [242, 35, 198, 137, 82, 225, 242, 182] Accounts: | Name | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | signer | Signer, Writable | User wallet | | mint | Account | Token mint | | bank | PDA, Writable | Target bank | | bank_token_account | PDA, Writable | Bank token vault | | user_account | PDA, Writable | User token state | | user_token_account | ATA, Writable | User's token account | | user_global_state | PDA, Writable | User global state | | Associated token and system programs | | | Arguments: * amount (u64): Amount to deposit ### withdraw Withdraws deposited tokens from a lending bank.

Discriminator: [183, 18, 70, 156, 148, 109, 161, 34] Accounts: Similar to deposit with reversed token flow Arguments: * amount (u64): Amount to withdraw ### borrow Borrows tokens against deposited collateral.

Discriminator: [228, 253, 131, 202, 207, 116, 89, 18] This instruction requires both borrow and collateral token accounts and their corresponding price feeds for risk calculations.

Key Accounts: 
* Borrow token bank and user accounts 
* Collateral token bank and user accounts 
* Pyth price feed accounts for both tokens 
* Borrow position account Arguments: * position_id (u64): Unique position identifier * amount (u64): Amount to borrow ### repay Repays borrowed tokens to close or reduce a borrow position.

Discriminator: [234, 103, 67, 82, 208, 234, 219, 166] Arguments: * position_id (u64): Position to repay * amount (u64): Repayment amount Sources: anchor/target/idl/lending.json9-1416 ## Account PriceFeedMessage Pyth Network price feed data structure used for asset valuation.

Fields: * feed_id ([u8; 32]): Pyth feed identifier * price (i64): Asset price * conf (u64): Price confidence interval * exponent (i32): Price decimal exponent * publish_time (i64): Price update timestamp * prev_publish_time (i64): Previous update timestamp * ema_price (i64): Exponential moving average price * ema_conf (u64): EMA confidence interval ### VerificationLevel Enum representing Pyth price update verification status: 
* Partial { num_signatures: u8 }: Partially verified with specified signature count 
* Full: Fully verified by two-thirds of guardians Sources: anchor/target/idl/lending.json1706-1933 ## Error Codes The program defines comprehensive error codes for different failure scenarios: | Code | Name | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | 6000 | BorrowAmountTooLarge | Borrow amount exceeds limits | | 6001 | WithdrawAmountExceedsCollateralValue | Withdrawal would break collateral requirements | | 6002 | OverWithdrawRequest | Withdrawal amount exceeds deposits | | 6003 | MathOverflow | Arithmetic overflow in calculations | | 6004 | OverBorrowRequest | Borrow amount exceeds available liquidity | | 6005 | OverRepayRequest | Repayment amount exceeds debt | | 6006 | HealthyAccount | Account is healthy (no liquidation needed) | | 6007 | OverBorrowableAmount | Borrow exceeds maximum borrowable amount | | 6008 | InvalidPriceFeed | Price feed validation failed | | 6009 | InvalidDepositAmount | Invalid deposit amount | | 6010 | InvalidWithdrawAmount | Invalid withdrawal amount | | 6011 | BorrowAmountTooSmall | Borrow amount below minimum | | 6012 | StalePrice | Price feed data is stale | | 6013 | InsufficientLiquidity | Insufficient bank liquidity | | 6014 | InsufficientFunds | User has insufficient funds | | 6015 | InsufficientCollateral | Insufficient collateral for operation | | 6016 | AlreadyRepayed | Position already fully repaid | Sources: anchor/target/idl/lending.json1497-1583 anchor/programs/lending/src/error.rs1-40 ## Constants The program defines one constant: 
* MAXIMUM_AGE (u64): 10000 
- Maximum age in seconds for price feed data before considered stale Sources: anchor/target/idl/lending.json1935-1941

## Testing

It explains the integration test suite, test environment setup, and testing utilities used to validate the Solana lending program functionality.

For information about local development environment setup, see Local Environment Setup.

Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts34-131 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts133-151 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts153-182 ## Test Environment Configuration The test environment setup involves initializing a simulated Solana blockchain with necessary program accounts and external dependencies. | Component | Configuration | Purpose | | --- | --- | --- | | startAnchor | Empty program path, lending program ID | Creates isolated blockchain context | | BankrunProvider | Uses test context | Provides Anchor program interface | | PythSolanaReceiver | Devnet price feeds | Enables price oracle testing | | Token Mints | USDC (6 decimals), SOL (9 decimals) | Creates test assets | | Price Feed IDs | *
* 

**SOL:**
** 0xef0d8b6f..., *
* 

**USDC:**
** 0xeaa020c6... | Links to Pyth price data | The setup process includes copying live Pyth program accounts from devnet to ensure realistic price feed behavior during testing.

Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts316-358 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts406-458 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts544-602 ## Test Execution Scripts The testing infrastructure provides several npm scripts for running tests in different configurations: | Script | Command | Purpose | | --- | --- | --- | | anchor-test | cd anchor && anchor test | Run full Anchor test suite | | anchor-localnet | cd anchor && anchor localnet | Start local Solana validator for testing | | anchor-build | cd anchor && anchor build | Build program for testing | The anchor test command automatically handles building the program, starting a local validator, deploying the program, and running the test suite.

Sources: package.json6-10 ## Test Utilities and Helpers The test suite includes several utility components for managing the simulated blockchain environment: ### BankrunContextWrapper A wrapper class that provides connection management for the Bankrun testing environment, enabling interaction with the simulated blockchain through standard Solana web3.js interfaces.

Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts10 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts51-52 ### Token and Account Management The tests use spl-token-bankrun utilities for creating and managing SPL tokens: * createMint() 
- Creates new token mints with specified decimals * createAccount() 
- Creates token accounts for users * mintTo() 
- Mints tokens to accounts for testing Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts89-105 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts267-290 ### Price Feed Integration The test suite integrates with Pyth Network price feeds using live devnet data copied into the test environment, ensuring realistic price behavior during testing operations that depend on asset valuations.

Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts54-81 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts413-435 ## State Validation Tests The test suite includes comprehensive validation of program state through dedicated test cases that query and verify account data: 
* User Financial Profile 
- Validates UserGlobalState and UserTokenState accounts 
* User Deposits 
- Confirms deposit s and collateral tracking 
* Active Borrow Positions 
- Verifies BorrowPosition account data 
* Bank Financial Profiles 
- Validates bank state including utilization rates and interest calculations These validation tests ensure that all program operations correctly update the underlying account data and maintain consistent state across related accounts.

Sources: anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts544-602 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts604-650 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts652-692 anchor/tests/lending.spec.ts695-793

## Frontend API Reference

# Frontend API Reference Relevant source files src/components/add-bank/add-bank-data-access.tsx src/components/borrow/active-borrow-positions.tsx src/components/deposit-tokens/deposit-tokens-ui.tsx src/components/deposits/deposits-data-access.tsx src/components/deposits/deposits-ui.tsx src/components/markets/markets-data-access.tsx src/components/repay/repay-ui.tsx src/components/transaction-history/transaction-history-data-access.tsx src/components/transaction-history/transaction-history-ui.tsx This document provides a comprehensive reference for the React hooks, components, and utility functions that comprise the frontend API of the Stervault DeFi lending platform.

It covers the data access layer, UI components, transaction management, and integration patterns used throughout the application.

For information about the underlying Solana program structure, see Program IDL Reference.

For configuration and environment setup, see Configuration and Constants. ## Data Access Hooks The frontend uses a hook-based architecture with React Query for data fetching and state management.

All blockchain interactions are abstracted through custom hooks that provide loading states, error handling, and automatic refetching. ### Transaction History The transaction history system provides * DEPOSIT, WITHDRAW, BORROW, REPAY

**API:**
** useMarketsBanks() | Property | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | banks | UseQueryResult<BankData[]> | All available lending banks | | getProgramAccount | UseQueryResult | Program account information | | programId | PublicKey | Lending program ID | BankData Interface * publicKey: Bank account address * account: Bank configuration including rates, fees, limits * tokenInfo: Associated token metadata * apy: Calculated annual percentage yield APY Calculation The system uses compound interest calculation: APY = (1 + r_period)^periodsPerYear - 1 where r_period = annualRate / periodsPerYear.

**API:**
** useBankProgram() | Property | Type | Description | | --- | --- | --- | | initBank | UseMutationResult | Create new lending bank | | initUser | UseMutationResult | Initialize user account | | banks | UseQueryResult | Available banks | initBank Parameters * tokenMint: Token mint address 
* Interest rates: liquidationThreshold, liquidationBonus, maxLtv 
* Rates in basis points: depositInterestRate, borrowInterestRate 
* Metadata: name, description 
* Fees: depositFee, withdrawalFee, minDeposit * interestAccrualPeriod: Compounding period in seconds Sources: src/components/add-bank/add-bank-data-access.tsx21-258 ## Transaction Operations * Select active borrow position 
* Validate repayment amount against outstanding debt 
* Check user has sufficient token balance 
* Calculate partial vs full repayment 
* Execute repay transaction 
* Update position state Sources: src/components/repay/repay-ui.tsx128-680 ## UI Component Architecture The UI layer consists of specialized components that handle different aspects of the lending platform interface. ### Form Management All transaction forms use a consistent pattern with react-hook-form and zod validation: ``` * Define Zod schema for validation 
* Initialize form with useForm and zodResolver 
* Use <FormField> components for inputs 
* Handle submission with validation 
* Show confirmation step before transaction 
* Execute transaction and show results Sources: src/components/repay/repay-ui.tsx62-71 src/components/deposit-tokens/deposit-tokens-ui.tsx35-39 ### Transaction History UI The transaction history * Real-time across transaction IDs, types, and tokens 
* Date range filtering with calendar picker 
* Token and transaction type filters 
* CSV export of filtered results 
* Automatic detail loading for unknown transactions 
* Direct links to Solana Explorer Sources: src/components/transaction-history/transaction-history-ui.tsx1-588 ### Deposit Management UI ``` * React Query provides automatic error states 
* User-friendly error messages with retry buttons 
* Toast notifications for transaction errors 
* Fallback UI components for critical errors 
* Error boundaries prevent complete application crashes Loading States 
* Skeleton loaders for data fetching 
* Spinner overlays for transactions 
* Disabled states during operations 
* Progress indicators for multi-step flows Sources: src/components/transaction-history/transaction-history-ui.tsx388-415 src/components/deposits/deposits-ui.tsx378-415 ## Utility Functions and Helpers The frontend includes numerous utility functions for common operations. ### Formatting Functions | Function | Purpose | Usage | | --- | --- | --- | | formatNumber() | Number display with locale | Currency and token amounts | | formatUsd() | USD currency formatting | Price displays | | formatTokenAmount() | Token-specific decimal handling | Based on mint decimals | | formatDate() | Timestamp to readable date | Transaction timestamps | | formatPercent() | Percentage display | APY, fees, ratios | ### Data Processing | Function | Purpose | Example | | --- | --- | --- | | calculateUsdValue() | USD value calculation | depositAmount * pythPrice | | determineTransactionType() | Parse transaction type from discriminator | Anchor instruction parsing | | extractTokenDetails() | Extract token info from transaction | Parse pre/post token balances | | bnToNumber() | Convert Anchor BN to number | Safe conversion with overflow handling | ### Caching and Performance ``` * Transaction data cached for 15 minutes 
* React Query stale-while-revalidate pattern 
* Throttled requests to prevent rate limiting 
* Exponential backoff for failed requests 
* Pagination and lazy loading for large datasets Sources: src/components/transaction-history/transaction-history-data-access.tsx22-53 ## Configuration and Constants

# Configuration and Constants Relevant source files anchor/bun.lock anchor/price-feeds/pyth_mapping_devnet.json anchor/programs/lending/src/instructions/deposit.rs public/tokens_localnet.json src/app/deposit-tokens/layout.tsx temp.ts This document covers the configuration files, constants, and environment-specific settings used throughout the Stervault DeFi lending platform.

It details how tokens are configured, price feeds are mapped, build dependencies are managed, and how different deployment environments are handled.

For information about the lending program's account structures and state management, see Account Structure and State Management.

For deployment procedures and environment setup, see Deployment and Configuration. ## Token Configuration System The platform uses a comprehensive token configuration system that defines supported assets across different environments.

Each token configuration includes metadata, addresses, decimals, and associated price feed information. ``` #mermaid-kqhhvh9ubp{font-family:ui-sans-serif,-apple-system,system-ui,Segoe The token configuration structure includes essential properties for each supported asset: | Property | Type | Purpose | | --- | --- | --- | | address | string | Token mint address on Solana | | symbol | string | Token symbol (SOL, USDC, etc.) | | name | string | Full token name | | decimals | number | Token decimal precision | | logoURI | string | URL to token logo image | | pythPriceFeed | string | Associated Pyth price feed address | | tags | string[] | Classification tags (verified, strict, community) | | daily_volume | number | 24-hour trading volume | | extensions.coingeckoId | string | CoinGecko API identifier | The localnet configuration includes major tokens like SOL, USDC, USDT, and newer assets like TRUMP and JLP, each with complete metadata and price feed mappings.

ProgramLTV CalculationsLiquidation Logic ``` The price feed configuration maps each supported token to its Pyth price feed: ``` { "pythProgramId": "pythWSnswVUd12oZpeFP8e9CVaEqJg25g1Vtc2biRsT", "priceFeeds": { "SOL": { "symbol": "SOL", "feedId": "0xef0d8b6fda2ceba41da15d4095d1da392a0d2f8ed0c6c7bc0f4cfac8c280b56d", "address": "7UVimffxr9ow1uXYxsr4LHAcV58mLzhmwaeKvJ1pjLiE" } } } ``` Each price feed entry contains: * symbol: Token identifier matching the token configuration * feedId: Unique Pyth feed identifier for price queries * address: Solana account address for the price feed This configuration enables the lending program to fetch real-time price data for risk calculations and liquidations.

Configurationlayout.tsxmetadataProvider HierarchyReactQueryProviderClusterProviderSolanaProviderToastertitle: 'Deposit Tokens'descriptionlang: 'en' ``` The layout configuration establishes: 
* Page metadata for SEO and browser display 
* Provider hierarchy for blockchain connectivity 
* Global styling and component structure 
* Error handling and notification systems Key configuration elements: 
* ReactQueryProvider: Data fetching and caching 
* ClusterProvider: Solana cluster/network management 
* SolanaProvider: Wallet connection and transaction handling 
* Toaster: User notification system Sources: src/app/deposit-tokens/layout.tsx1-30 ## Constants and Addresses The system uses various constants for program addresses, configuration values, and system
